= 0;\n      sent: Record<string, unknown>[] = [];\n      constructor(readonly url: string) { super(); }\n      send(text: string, written?: () => void): void { this.sent.push(JSON.parse(text)); Buffer.from(''));\n      }\n      terminate(): void { this.close(1006); }\n    }\n\n    function setup() {\n      vi.useFakeTimers();\n      vi.spyOn(Math, 'random').mockReturnValue(0.5);\n      const type Handlers = Record<string, (...args: any[]) => void>;\n    let sockets: Array<{ handlers: Handlers; send: ReturnType<typeof vi.fn> }>;\n\n    /** Connected on a second socket, with the first's 'server', color: 'green', publicKey: 'old-key', pairedAt: '2024-01-01', lastSeen: '2024-01-01',\n      });\n      vi.mocked(WsMock).mockImplementation(function (this: any) {\n        const handlers: Handlers = {};\n        this.readyState = 1;\n        this.socket = makeMockSocket(FINGERPRINT);\n        this.on = vi.fn().mockImplementation((event: string, cb: any) => {\n          handlers[event] = cb;\n          if (event === 'open') setTimeout(cb, 0);\n          return this;\n        });\n        this.send = vi.fn(); this.ping = vi.fn(); this.close = vi.fn(); this.terminate = vi.fn();   const response = Object.assign(new EventEmitter(), { statusCode: status });\n          const request = { destroy: vi.fn() };\n          socket.handlers['unexpected-response'](request, response);\n          return { request, finish: async (error: string) => {\n            response.emit('data', Buffer.from(JSON.stringify({ error })));\n            response.emit('end');\n            await
